# NBI2 TX2 native bridge inventory

**Modelling gaps first:** the adopted headline still takes `IDa_Deployed.Uninstalled.adapter`. Reached ASQ8/request, registry/PDA/account, scan/reconstruction and emission boundaries include abstract operations; name-matching another extraction does not install it. In particular `emit_result` is only `AdapterRw.Ops.op53` in this closure. Its full native Result/ASR8/set-return-data behavior is not represented by a concrete executable `emit_result` definition here. The separate `JHb_EmitAdapter.source` fixture installation is outside the headline kernel dependency set and supplies no credit to this inventory.

The JSON flags **47 rows** in these boundary groups, including their codec helpers. These are proposed native-bridge obligations, not newly discovered shipped bugs, failed security theorems, or additional cryptographic assumptions. A helper with no separate cohort definition can instead be modeled inside a named caller; such rows cite that context and are not automatically called gaps. Entry dispatch, return-data clear/set, exact SDK snapshot/cleanup and syscall association remain explicit runtime/extraction work.

| Boundary | Exact adopted representation | Required native bridge |
|---|---|---|
| ASQ8 decoder / registry / policy | `AdapterRw.Ops.op23`, `op15`, `op25`–`op34` | Validate exact request/version/flags, reserved bytes, payment and current-slot domains; retain rejected prefixes. |
| Pool IDs / account decoders | `AdapterRw.Ops.op45`–`op46`, `op48`–`op50` | Frozen default/devnet deployment constants, saved-bump PDA derivation, canonical master/checkpoint/lane bytes and frontier invariant. |
| Proof buffer / ASF8 reconstruction | `AdapterRw.Ops.op51`–`op52`; `op22` is concretely installed as `AspisS6CurrentCodecInstallation.encode` | Same 58295-byte proof account, 688-byte afterstate and 57607-byte suffix; same authenticated 1880-byte serialization used in resume. |
| ASR8 emission | `AdapterRw.Ops.op53` | Same accepted statement, full result validation/encoding, exact magic/length, ordered runtime return-data write. |
| SDK second wrapper / entry | `AspisR1.S2W_D189Wrappers.second`, `S2W_D189Entry.Entry.snapshot`; native/extraction inputs retained | Seven same-account snapshots, allocator cleanup, observer None and Clock/return-data effects; preserve metadata and same saved checkpoint. |

## Claim, pins and scope

This is a source-reading inventory and bridge plan, not a proof or premise removal. **S2 and the deployed 100-bit target remain not established.** Headline: `AspisR1.RPx_Deployed.deployed`; L154 remains a statement parameter. Narrowed D188, frozen3, the approved two-transaction game and `RuntimePersistence` are unchanged. No XRn_Join kit or stand-in is used.

Frozen source: `4bb3b3f51e16b4525f1d10336e2b0bcbbd775387`. All bodies are read using `git show`/`git cat-file` at that revision. SHA256 includes the complete `{...}` body, including disabled branches, byte-for-byte; JSON gives byte offsets and full-file hashes. The two `FibreIndex` macro-generated methods hash their verbatim method templates and cite the invocation/bound. Anonymous closures are merged into the enclosing caller and its hash; local closure calls are distinguished from repository functions.

Release feature evidence is reused read-only from `evidence/lowering2-20261026/Low2_build-default-v2_command.json` and `Low2_build-devnet-v1_command.json`: R1 program `pool-adapter,private-leaf-salts` plus default custom heap; its library selects optimized-terminal/program-build. Default/devnet differ in compile-time Pool/registry IDs, not call structure. No audit-eight-way, prover, profiler, test or Kani branch is reached. Both registry versions and both payment variants are included. Non-Pool legacy `[2]` and strict `tx2` are outside these release invocations. `S2LeadInterface.dataWrite` explicitly puts Pool settlement/CPI effects in a separate caller cone; this inventory ends at the verifier’s ASR8 return-data boundary, rather than claiming to inventory the Pool processor itself.

Retained artifact pins, **not reproduced here**: default `f8c0ec72621fd5029a97d796c4a3623d0dc44a8e7074122372f9872915d143d4`; devnet `63ad3e9cc096dcbb55b3ca7ac7c8e89e6a845796ab5e4f5b7d15db96f3784299`; Pool `121f3e29741f510e6dc42d0345fc3c36217600f0be337094855c4a9da2581548`. No shipped/cfg(kani) change occurred, so the rebuild trigger did not fire.

## Adopted cohort evidence and inventory limits

The constant set is `evidence/replay-deployed2-20261027/RPx_reachable-constants.json`, rooted at the actual theorem, with `RPx_import-closure-check.json` and `evidence/replay-tops3-20261027/RPv_FJv_Top-import-closure-check.json`. Source selection uses `RPv_plan.json`’s logical-to-replayed module map, overridden by the RPx plan. Every listed cohort name is in that retained kernel dependency set. Each definition below cites its selected source file, module and line; JSON pins its source hash. No new Lean environment is claimed replayed. Named counterparts can have different carrier names and require the stated bridge; same spelling alone is not correspondence evidence.

The inventory has **242 entries across 29 frozen files**, with **73 entries having no separately named counterpart**. This is a syntax-tree call inventory with explicit source/feature/type resolutions, not compiler-derived exact reachability. Arithmetic method calls conservatively list the canonical field-carrier alternatives in JSON; the caller bridge must resolve the actual receiver type and its domain. Consequently the count is an inventory size, not an exact dynamic call count or a claim that every conservative field alternative executes. All source call sites (also rejected/disabled branches) are preserved and classified in JSON; the Markdown lists active callees and external/runtime boundaries. Runtime S2/Arity::Two specializations exclude unused generic branches from the bridge graph, while retaining them in body hashes.

## Accepted TX2 order and CLOSED leaves

`process_r1_cost_probe_instruction` → `pool_route::admitted` → adapter `process` → owned `stage2` (clear return data; seven snapshots; Clock) → `stage2_owned` → `validate_asq8` (decode/authenticate/scan/reconstruct) → encode ASF8 → `tx2_authenticated` → `parse_shape` → `tx2_common` → `resume` (proof,public,checkpoint-checksum digest order; read Prepared/Accumulator) → require next=10 → powers29 → batch1 three-tree authentication → geometry10..22/pending → twelve query steps → pending.finish → relation rounds/Pair/final dot → `emit_result`.

TX2 never calls `Prepared::new` or `prepared_claim`. Those CLOSED preparation leaves provide upstream domains through the SAME saved checkpoint; do not invoke their fresh-producer hypotheses as if TX2 reran them. `resume` checks the checksum but byte identity/provenance across transactions still uses the approved persistence/game/account association. Strict TX2 canonical_scan is absent; consumer checks or retained TX1 canonicality must establish every callee domain.

The private `canonical::Dot::push` bridge is `LPn.Dot.native_result` (`lean/LPn_DotClosed.lean`): SAME four-u64 state/operands and full Result/void port; its local wrapping theorem does not require an aggregate no-wrap/count premise. The native operand producers still establish canonical private Q values. The public accumulator caller must prove count≤4096 and the sum/product invariant before finish. See LPn_STUBS.md/LPh_STUBS.md; no new stub is introduced here.

## Proposed bridge units, bottom up

S/M/L are planning estimates, not measurements or closure claims. Every unit below is OPEN as an enclosing native/cohort bridge unless a narrower CLOSED leaf is explicitly cited. Small getters, local closures and source helpers belong to the listed caller unit. Every edge is a proof obligation on the SAME actual call arguments/state; every loop uses one native step plus Lean induction. Multiplication uses paired Lean-discharged stubs only.
